```text
2. Try using the network_turbo with HF mirror


3. Try a different download strategy

The CLAUDE.md documentation recommends ModelScope for large model downloads since AutoDL's HuggingFace proxy is notoriously slow, but ModelScope was also crawling at similar speeds. This suggests the bottleneck might be with this specific AutoDL instance's network. Maybe aria2c with multiple concurrent connections could help bypass the throttling.

I should also verify whether combining `network_turbo` with hf-mirror is creating a conflict—the docs say to enable network_turbo before pip/git operations, but hf-mirror is already a China CDN, so they might be interfering with each other. Let me try aria2c for parallel downloads instead, which should give much better throughput.
```

```json
{
  "server": "westd-32608",
  "command": "mkdir -p /root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B && cat > /tmp/download_aria2.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nDEST=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B\"\nBASE=\"https://hf-mirror.com/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B/resolve/main\"\n\n# Download small files first\nfor f in config.json generation_config.json model.safetensors.index.json tokenizer.json tokenizer_config.json; do\n    aria2c -x 4 -s 4 -d \"$DEST\" -o \"$f\" \"$BASE/$f\" 2>&1 | tail -1\ndone\n\n# Download large safetensors files with 16 connections each\necho \"=== Downloading model-00001-of-000002.safetensors ===\"\naria2c -x 16 -s 16 -k 50M -d \"$DEST\" -o \"model-00001-of-000002.safetensors\" \"$BASE/model-00001-of-000002.safetensors\" 2>&1 | grep -E \"Download complete|ETA|ERR\"\n\necho \"=== Downloading model-00002-of-000002.safetensors ===\"\naria2c -x 16 -s 16 -k 50M -d \"$DEST\" -o \"model-00002-of-000002.safetensors\" \"$BASE/model-00002-of-000002.safetensors\" 2>&1 | grep -E \"Download complete|ETA|ERR\"\n\necho \"=== All downloads complete ===\"\nls -lh \"$DEST\"/\necho \"EXIT_CODE=$?\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /tmp/download_aria2.sh\nnohup bash /tmp/download_aria2.sh > /tmp/download_deepseek_llama8b.log 2>&1 &\necho \"PID=$!\"",

}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The download is proceeding very slowly - about 400KB/s for each file. This means about 5-7 hours for the full download. There are two issues:

1. The small files' downloads were started. The first download method (small files + aria2c) seems to have completed ("download completed" for 2 files).
2. But the huggingface_hub download (PID 190333) that I killed earlier also seems to have respawned... Wait, no - looking at the log, there's a modelscope lock message. 

Actually I see that the aria2c is not being used here - the download shows "Downloading [model-00001..." which is the huggingface_hub format, not aria2c. 

So aria2c handled the small files but then the HF downloader took over for the rest. The real problem is the speed - 400KB/s is unacceptable. I need to kill everything, clean up, and switch to using aria2c directly with multiple connections on the large files instead of routing through huggingface_hub, pointing it at the mirror URLs.

Looking at the logs more carefully, aria2c finished the small files successfully, but then something triggered the modelscope lock and started single-connection downloads again - I need to figure out what's actually running right now and why the multi-connection approach isn't being used.
```

</details>

```json
{
  "server": "westd-32608",
  "command": "cat > /tmp/dl_aria2.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nDEST=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B\"\nBASE=\"https://hf-mirror.com/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B/resolve/main\"\n\necho \"$(date): Starting downloads...\"\n\n# Small files sequentially\nfor f in config.json generation_config.json model.safetensors.index.json tokenizer.json tokenizer_config.json; do\n    echo \"Downloading $f...\"\n    aria2c -x 4 -s 4 -q -d \"$DEST\" -o \"$f\" \"$BASE/$f\"\ndone\necho \"$(date): Small files done\"\n\n# Two large files in parallel, each with 16 connections\necho \"$(date): Starting safetensors downloads...\"\naria2c -x 16 -s 16 -k 20M --file-allocation=none -d \"$DEST\" -o \"model-00001-of-000002.safetensors\" \"$BASE/model-00001-of-000002.safetensors\" &\nPID1=$!\naria2c -x 16 -s 16 -k 20M --file-allocation=none -d \"$DEST\" -o \"model-00002-of-000002.safetensors\" \"$BASE/model-00002-of-000002.safetensors\" &\nPID2=$!\n\necho \"PID1=$PID1, PID2=$PID2\"\nwait $PID1\necho \"$(date): model-00001 done (exit=$?)\"\nwait $PID2\necho \"$(date): model-00002 done (exit=$?)\"\n\necho \"$(date): ALL DONE\"\nls -lh \"$DEST\"/\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /tmp/dl_aria2.sh\nnohup bash /tmp/dl_aria2.sh > /tmp/dl_deepseek.log 2>&1 &\necho \"PID=$!\"",
  "account": "root",
  "warn": true
}
```
